HFT visibility is governed by the authoring policy above.","docket_mode":"quarantine_without_hft","docket_ready":true}},"selected_context_units":[],"support_registry":[{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_b:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_1de00e0c79fdb6647efa","text":"1. **Avoidance as moving to the side of reminder.** Lexical evidence: جنب B003 keeping aside/avoiding/distance; شقو B002 hardship/strenuous suffering. Structural cue: the pronoun \"ها\" points back to الذكرى, and the verse stands opposite 87:10. Assigned role: جنب describes spatial-moral withdrawal; شقو names the one defined by hardship. Abductive move: the packet supplies avoidance and hardship; I infer that avoiding reminder causes or reveals wretchedness. Reading change: the ayah is not passive unbelief but active sidestepping of a beneficial summons.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000262/B011"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-3","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_23b6845759cc79d3c963","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"87:11:1\",\"root_000262\",[\"B011\"]]],\"grade\":\"reject\",\"text\":\"Öğüdü yana itmek, koruyucu bir kalkan kurma girişimi gibi görünse de sonraki ateş bu korumanın kişiyi açıkta bıraktığını gösterir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000262/B003","root_000808/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_2cf4d21a551469968f8c","text":"1. **Avoidance is active spatial self-placement.** `ج ن ب/B003` gives keeping aside, distancing, and withdrawal; `ش ق و/B002` gives hardship and strenuous suffering. The pronoun ties the movement to the reminder, and 87:12–13 immediately extends that sideways move into fire and a state with neither death nor life. Avoidance supplies the initial displacement; hardship supplies the resulting course. I infer the directional arrow from side-stepping to outcome. The ayah reads as self-routing away from an available address, not passive failure to hear.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000262/B011"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_3bd018706997e3007bde","text":"3. **Avoidance behaves like an attempted shield that reverses into exposure.** `ج ن ب/B011` is a shield or cover held at the side. The root’s protective image is activated by the immediate movement into `ٱلنَّارَ ٱلْكُبْرَىٰ`: moving the reminder aside can be modeled as attempted self-protection, but the sequel reveals that maneuver as ineffective. The packet supplies avoidance and fire; I supply the protective intention and its reversal. The ayah thus permits an ironic reading in which the chosen “shield” is precisely what leaves the person exposed.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000262/B011","root_000808/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_4e98dec41cefbad08b84","text":"87:12 immediately overturned the protective possibility of `ج ن ب/B011`: the attempted side-shield becomes exposure to fire. 87:13 then made `ش ق و/B002` causal rather than classificatory, because the spatial act of avoidance opens into hardship that has neither terminal closure nor beneficial life.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000262/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_5b598980abd2ecbeffb4","text":"2. **The same side-image exposes failed proximity.** `ج ن ب/B002` gives nearness or companionship at one’s side, whereas the surface form selects `ج ن ب/B003`, withdrawal. The reminder has just been made present in 87:9–10, so the root holds adjacency and separation in direct tension. Its role is to show that distance is produced in the presence of something near. I infer availability from the preceding imperative and response. The reading changes from absence of guidance to a deliberate rupture of proximity.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000262/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_677fc6bdec9b1b4ae002","text":"1. **Avoidance as lateral self-removal.** Lexical evidence: ج ن ب B003 (keeping aside/avoidance), ش ق و B002 (hardship and strenuous suffering). Structural cue: 87:10 receptive remembrance is immediately contrasted with the one who avoids it. Assigned role: يتجنب names the body's movement to the side; الأشقى names the hardship-state that results. Abductive move: moving aside from reminder is not neutral distance but the beginning of hardship. Assigned role: شقو's mountain/hardship branches supply a parody of ascent: climbing into hardship rather than rising by praise.
